HudBot 0.5 - Supported Texture Replacements

Some .bmps are not in because they had duplicate
CRCs. Most of these are duplicated tiles or blank frames
from animations.

To replace a .BMP, simple place a .TGA with the same
name in the "Tribes/HudBot/Replacements" directory,
or in a .ZIP located in "Tribes/HudBot/Replacements"

For Example, to use a custom disc skin, you would create
"Tribes/HudBot/Replacements/disc.tga", or put disc.tga 
in a .ZIP that is in "Tribes/HudBot/Replacements/*.ZIP"

Custtom terrain IS supported.

See Docs/replacements.html for information on how to add your
own replacements.
